\begin{document}
\begin{center}{\LARGE al-Khwārizmī}\\[3mm]
{\Large \textit{The Book of the Image of the Earth}}\\[2mm]
{\large (\textit{Kitāb ṣūrat al-arḍ})}\\[4mm]
English translation of the Arabic text in the edition of Hans von Mžik\\
(Leipzig: Otto Harrassowitz, 1926; reprinted Baghdad, 1962)\end{center}
\medskip
{\small Every line of the edition is translated, in its order, from the line-by-line transcription in this repository
(\texttt{../anchors.tsv}): the title pages, the headings, the tables of the cities and of the mountains, the
descriptions of the seas, the islands, the boundaries of the countries and the rivers, and the index. The pages of the
edition stand in the margin; where a page ends inside a paragraph, the mark follows the last point of the page. As in
the edition, the names of the mountains stand on one page and their coordinates, row by row in the same order, on the
next; the headings of these tables run across both pages, and their halves are marked with \dots.

The numerals of al-Khwārizmī, letters with their abjad values, are given as figures, in degrees and minutes. Two
letters without their dots allow more than one value (\textarabic{ڡ} 80 or 100; \textarabic{ٮ} 2, 10 or 50). Where the
neighbouring points of the same entry, or else of the next entries, decide clearly between the readings, the nearest
is given; in the tables of the cities and in the headings of the climes, the band of the clime decides the latitude. Otherwise every value is given, as
88/108\textdegree. A group of letters that is no abjad number stays in Arabic letters. Most names stand in Arabic
letters, as the edition prints them; familiar ones are given in transliteration or in their English form.

The edition's apparatus: its running numbers stand in parentheses; the numbers in square brackets are those of the
same items in Suhrāb's \textit{ʿAjāʾib al-aqālīm al-sabʿa}, as Mžik gives them; angle brackets enclose what Mžik
supplies. Where his supplement begins or ends inside a word, the brackets stand on the whole words it covers; a
supplement within a single word is not marked. A figure that Mžik supplies wholly or in part stands in angle
brackets. Obvious misprints of the running numbers and of the brackets are corrected. Mžik's note marks are not
reproduced, and his German introduction and footnotes are not translated: they are his own writing, protected until
the end of 2031. The lettered footnotes are the translator's: they record misprints of al-Khwārizmī's text, the
editor's corrections of it that the translation follows (his additions and corrections, p.~163), and readings that
stay open. The qualifier \textit{mukhayyar} (\textarabic{مخيّر}) of some colours of the mountains is left
untranslated; its sense here is not certain.

AI-translated (Claude), 2026, from the transcription in this repository; no person has proofread it.
al-Khwārizmī's text is in the public domain. To the extent that any rights exist in this AI-produced translation,
they are dedicated to the public domain under CC0 1.0. The dedication does not concern Mžik's edition or its German
matter.}
\bigskip

\Pg{p. 42}The description of the mountain that surrounds the Island of the Ruby, which lies at the beginning of the boundary of the east, in the farthest part of the Sea of China, \Ang{\langle}which is\Ang{\rangle} the Dark Sea, on which no one sails. A mountain surrounding the Island of the Ruby begins at longitude~167°~0′ and latitude~14°~30′ (596) [1457], passing to latitude~9°~0′ in a straight line (597) [1458]; then it turns round to latitude~5°~0′ and longitude~170°~0′ (598) [1459]; it passes on and enters the Dark Sea at latitude~4°~0′ and longitude~172°~0′ (599) [1460], and enters the sea at latitude~8°~30′ and longitude~178°~30′ (600) [1461]; then it surrounds the island in the shape of a basket; then the mountain comes.

\par\medskip\begin{center}\bfseries The mountains that are in …\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The mountains that are in …}{F048-L08}\par

\Pg{p. 43}Along longitude~178°~30′ until it ends at the equator (601) [1462]; then it turns round to longitude~176°~30′, the latitude being 3°~15/55′ beyond the equator (602) [1463]; then it turns round to longitude~179°~0′, touching the equator (603) [1464]; then it passes along this longitude until the latitude, in the first clime, is 6°~0′ (604) [1465]; then it turns round until the latitude is 9°~30′ and the longitude~176°~30′ (605) [1466]; then it turns round until the longitude is 178°~0′ and the latitude~5°~30′ (606) [1467]. Its colour is red; it is eastern, the mountain begins towards the east and passes round the island, and this is its figure.

\par\medskip\begin{center}\Pg{p. 66}\bfseries The seas on the globe of the inhabited earth \textarabic{وجزائرها}\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The seas on the globe of the inhabited earth وجزائرها}{F072-L01}\par

\par\medskip\begin{center}\bfseries The outer western sea and the outer northern sea\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The outer western sea and the outer northern sea}{F072-L02}\par

Its beginning is from longitude~1°~0′ and latitude~0°~10′ (967) [503]; it passes to longitude~20°~0′ and latitude~0°~10′ (968) [504]; and passes in the shape of a ṭaylasān to longitude~17°~0′ and latitude~8°~0′ (969) [505]; and passes to longitude~9°~0′ and latitude~8°~30′ (970) [506]; and passes in the shape of a qawwāra to longitude~7°~0′ and latitude~12°~30′ (971) [507]; then it passes to longitude~9°~45′ and latitude~12°~20′ (972) [508]; then it passes to longitude~9°~45′ and latitude~16°~0′ (973) [509]; and passes to longitude~10°~0′ and latitude~16°~0′ (974); it passes to longitude~10°~0′ and latitude~17°~0′ (975) [560]; then it passes in the shape of the shābūra to longitude~9°~20′ and latitude~18°~10′ (976) [561], and there is the mouth of the river \textarabic{روذس}; and passes to longitude~9°~50′ and latitude~19°~40′ (977) [562]; and turns back like a shābūra to longitude~9°~15/55′ and latitude~21°~0′ (978) [563]; and passes to longitude~9°~45′, and there is the mouth of the river \textarabic{حوسدس}, and latitude~21°~45′ (979) [564]; and passes to longitude~9°~10′ and latitude~28°~0′ (980) [565]; and passes to longitude~9°~0′ and latitude~26°~0′ (981) [566]; and passes to longitude~8°~0′ and latitude~28°~0′ (982) [567]; and passes to longitude~\Ang{\langle}9°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~28°~0′ (983) [568]\Pg{p. 67}; and passes to longitude~8°~10′ and latitude~29°~10′ (984) [569]; it passes to longitude~8°~0′ and latitude~31°~0′ (985) [570]; it passes to longitude~8°~30′ and latitude~32°~0′ (986) [571]; and passes in the shape of a qawwāra to longitude~7°~30′ and latitude~35°~40′ (987) [572]: that is at Tangier. Then it leaves a space of two thirds of a degree from this place, and that is on the line of the clime whose latitude is 36°~0′ (988). This place is joined to the Sea of Tangier; on it there is the figure of two idols of copper that hold up another idol by its feet with their hands, and it is said that these are the landmarks of Hercules, which no one has passed. It passes that \Ang{\langle}place\Ang{\rangle} and comes to longitude~5°~50′ and latitude~36°~20′ (989) [573]; it passes to longitude~7°~15/55′ and latitude~37°~10′ (990) [574]; and passes to longitude~5°~0′ and latitude~37°~10′ (991) [575]; it passes to longitude~4°~0′ and latitude~37°~40′ (992) [576]; it passes to longitude~4°~30′, and there is the mouth of the river \textarabic{اس}, and latitude~38°~45′ (993) [577]; it passes to longitude~4°~10′ and latitude~38°~45′ (994) [578]; it passes to longitude~6°~30′ and latitude~39°~15/55′ (995) [579]; it passes to longitude~5°~30′ and latitude~39°~40′ (996) [580]; it passes to longitude~6°~40′ and latitude~41°~0′ (997) [581]; it passes to longitude~6°~0′ and latitude~41°~15/55′ (998) [582]; it passes to longitude~6°~45′ and latitude~42°~0′ (999) [583]; it passes to longitude~6°~40′ and latitude~48°~0′ (1000) [584]; it passes to longitude~6°~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~48°~15′ (1001) [585]; The land of the forest of \textarabic{ارقونيوس}, its middle is at longitude~41°~0′ and latitude~58°~0′ (1595) [544].

The land of \textarabic{سرماطيه}, which is the land of the Burjān, its middle is at longitude~45°~0′ and latitude~52°~0′ (1596) [545].

The land of \textarabic{الٮتز}, its middle is at longitude~57°~0′ and latitude~58°~0′ (1597) [551].

The island of \textarabic{طوقيا}, which is joined to \textarabic{التيز}, its middle is at longitude~58°~30′ and latitude~53°~30′ (1598) [552].

The land of \textarabic{سرماطيه}, which is the land of the Alans, its middle is at longitude~78°~0′ and latitude~59°~30′ (1599) [546].

The land of \textarabic{اسقونيا}, which is the land of the Turks, its middle is at longitude~104°~0′ and latitude~57°~30′ (1600) [547].

The land of \textarabic{اسقوثيا}, which is the land of the Toghuzghuz, its middle is at longitude~148°~0′ and latitude~59°~30′ (1601) [548].

The land of \textarabic{سيرقى}, which is the land of \textarabic{سينستان}, its middle is at longitude~160°~0′ and latitude~54°~30′ (1602) [549].

The land of Magog at longitude~171°~30′ and latitude~58°~0′ (1603) [550].

\par\medskip\begin{center}\Pg{p. 106}\bfseries \textarabic{ار\AngOpen{}. . . . .\AngClose{}ا} \Ang{\langle}the springs\Ang{\rangle} that are beyond the equator\footnoteT{The first word is printed with a gap of dots in angle brackets, as the editor leaves a lacuna of the manuscript; it is not restored here.}\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{ارAngOpen. . . . .AngCloseا Anglanglethe springsAngrangle th}{F112-L01}\par

A spring comes out of the mountain \textarabic{الّهه} from \Ang{\langle}beyond\Ang{\rangle} the equator from longitude~30°~30′ and latitude~5°~0′ (1604) [2230]; and passes to longitude~27°~0′ and latitude~8°~30′ (1605) [2231]; then it passes to the sea in the first clime at longitude~19°~0′ and latitude~6°~10′ (1606) [2232].

\Ang{\langle}The Nile of Egypt and the springs and the rivers that fall into it\Ang{\rangle}.

Two round marshes, the diameter of each five degrees: the centre of the first is at longitude~50°~0′ and latitude~7°~0′ (1607) [1503], and the centre of the second at longitude~57°~0′ and latitude~7°~0′ (1608) [1504].

Into the first there pour five rivers from the Mountain of the Moon: the beginning of the first river is at longitude~48°~0′ (1609) [1505], the second at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~49°~0′ (1610) [1506], the third at longitude~50°~0′ (1611) [1507], the fourth at longitude~51°~0′ (1612) [1508] and the fifth at longitude~52°~0′ (1613) [1509].

From the Mountain of the Moon there also come out five rivers to the second marsh: the beginning of the first river is at longitude~55°~20′ (1614) [1510], the second at 56°~20′ (1615) [1511], the third at 57°~20′ (1616) [1512], the fourth at 58°~20′ (1617) [1513]\Pg{p. 107} and the fifth at 59°~20′ (1618) [1514].

From these two marshes there come out, from each of them, four rivers to a round marsh in the first clime; the diameter of this marsh is two degrees, and its centre is at longitude~58°~30′ and latitude, in the first clime, 2°~0′ (1619) [1515].

The beginning of the first river from the first marsh is at longitude~48°~40′ (1620) [1516], the second at longitude~49°~30′ (1621) [1517] and the third at longitude~51°~15′ (1622) [1518]; the second and the third join at longitude~52°~0′ and latitude~1°~0′ beyond the equator (1623) [1519]; then it passes to the marsh that we have mentioned as one river; and the fourth river is at longitude~52°~0′ (1624) [1520].

And of the rivers that come from the second marsh to the small one, the beginning of the first is likewise at longitude~55°~30′ (1625) [1521], the second at longitude~56°~20′ (1626) [1522] and the third at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~58°~0′ (1627) [1523]; the second and the third meet at longitude~56°~0′ and latitude~1°~0′ beyond the equator (1628) [1524]; then it passes to the marsh that we have mentioned as one river; and the fourth river is at longitude~59°~0′ (1629) [1525]. All these rivers come to the small marsh, the mouth of each not touching the other.

Then \Ang{\langle}there comes out of this small marsh a great river, which is the Nile\Ang{\rangle} of Egypt. It passes by the land of the Blacks, \Ang{\langle}ʿAlwa and Zaghāwa\Ang{\rangle}, Fazzān and Nubia, and passes by Dunqula, the city of Nubia, at longitude~52°~20′ and latitude~2°~0′ from the first clime, counted from beyond it towards the equator, or, from the equator to it, 15°~0′ (1630) [1526]\Pg{p. 108}. The Nile passes on and cuts the line of the first clime at longitude~53°~0′ and latitude~16°~20′ (1631) [1527], and it passes on until it goes beyond the line of the.

First clime by a degree and a half in its direction (1632) [1528]; then it turns aside to longitude~52°~0′ and latitude~18°~40′ (1633) [1529]; then it turns aside to longitude~51°~0′ and latitude~17°~0′ (1634) [1530]; then it turns aside to longitude~50°~0′ and latitude~17°~30′ (1635) [1531]; then it turns aside to longitude~50°~20′ and latitude~18°~20′ (1636) [1532]; then it turns aside to longitude~52°~30′ and latitude~19°~40′ (1637) [1533]; then it passes and comes to the city of \textarabic{ملوى} at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~19°~20′ (1638) [1534]; then it passes and comes to the city of Aswān, touching it, at longitude~55°~30′ and latitude~22°~30′ (1639) [1535]; then it turns aside \Ang{\langle}to longitude\Ang{\rangle}~58°~0′ and latitude~24°~0′ (1640) [1536], touching the mountain \textarabic{اليوڡا}; then it turns aside to longitude~55°~30′ and latitude~24°~20′ (1641) [1537]; then it passes and falls in the third clime, touching in some places the mountain al-Muqaṭṭam and the cities on it, whose longitude and latitude we have written down; then it passes to Miṣr, touching it, at longitude~54°~30′ and latitude~29°~15′ (1642) [1538]; then it branches off from Miṣr to the sea in seven channels.

The first of them passes to Alexandria at longitude~51°~30′ (1643) [1539].

From this channel there come out two channels, the first of them at longitude~51°~40′ (1644) [1540] and the other at longitude~58°~0′ (1645) [1541]\Pg{p. 109}.

And the mouth of the second channel that comes out of Miṣr to the sea is at longitude~53°~0′ (1646) [1542], the third at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~58°~30′ (1647) [1543], the fourth at longitude~58°~40′ (1648) [1544], the fifth at longitude~58°~50′ (1649) [1545], the sixth at longitude~54°~20′ (1650) [1546] and the seventh at longitude~54°~30′ (1651) [1547]; and there is Damietta.

The description of the spring on the equator that flows into the Nile of Egypt.

A round spring, its centre on the equator and its outflow into the Nile at the city of Nubia; the diameter of this spring is three degrees, and its centre is at longitude~62°~0′ (1652) [1548]. A river comes out of it at longitude~61°~30′ (1653) [1549]; its mouth is in the Nile at longitude~58°~0′ and latitude~16°~20′ (1654) [1550], touching.

The first clime; and there is a channel between the river of this spring and the Nile, above the city of Nubia.

A spring in the Island of Silver in the Sea of China, at longitude~154°~40′ and latitude~8°~30′ beyond the equator (1655) [2233]. Three rivers come out of it from one place; the middle one of them passes by the corner of the city that is on the island and passes to the sea at longitude~159°~40′ and latitude~4°~30′ (1656) [2234]; the first river flows into the sea at longitude~154°~40′ and latitude~5°~20′ (1657) [2235], and the third flows into the sea at longitude~159°~50′ and latitude~7°~45′ (1658) [2236].

A spring in the Island of Silver in the Dark Sea, at longitude~172°~30′ and latitude~5°~20′ beyond the equator (1659) [2237]. Two rivers come out of it, both flowing into the sea: the mouth of the first is at longitude~169°~40′ and latitude~8°~20′ (1660) [2238], and the second at longitude~178°~40′ and latitude~6°~40′ (1661) [2239].

\par\medskip\begin{center}\Pg{p. 110}\bfseries The first clime and the springs and the rivers that are in it\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The first clime and the springs and the rivers that are in i}{F116-L01}\par

A spring in the mountain \textarabic{قاقس} a river, \textarabic{درطوس}, comes out of it flows into the sea the beginning of the river at longitude~26°~30′ and latitude~11°~0′ (1662) [1684] and its end is at longitude~10°~20′ and latitude~14°~0′ (1663) [1685].

A spring in the mountain \textarabic{ثلا} a river comes out of it its mouth is in the river \textarabic{درطوس}, its beginning is at longitude~37°~30′ and latitude~9°~40′ (1664) [1686]; and passes to longitude~28°~0′ and latitude~7°~30′ (1665) [1687]; then it passes to longitude~21°~45′ bending a little to the city of \textarabic{ثمنورٮقى} and latitude~7°~0′ (1666) [1688]; then it passes and flows into the river \textarabic{درطوس} at longitude~7°~0′ and latitude~12°~30′ (1667) [1689].

A spring in the second clime; a river comes out of it and flows into this \Ang{\langle}river; its beginning is at\Ang{\rangle} longitude \textarabic{ا\AngOpen{}د\AngClose{}} \Ang{\langle}30′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~7°~20′ (1668) [1690] \Ang{\langle}and its mouth is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~\Ang{\langle}28°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~17°~20′ (1669) [1691].

A spring falls also into the first river, its beginning is in the mountain \textarabic{سرعاطوس} at longitude~29°~30′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~20°~0′\Ang{\rangle} (1670) [1692]; it passes to a small marsh whose centre is at longitude~28°~0′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~18°~30′\Ang{\rangle} (1671) [1693]; and passes and falls into the river that we have described at longitude~27°~30′ and latitude~17°~30′ (1672) [1694]\Pg{p. 111}.

A spring in the mountain flows into the first river also the beginning of the spring at longitude~24°~0′ and latitude~20°~30′ (1673) [1695], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~26°~20′ and latitude~18°~20′ (1674) [1696]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{بغيرا}.

And a spring also flows into the first river, its beginning is at longitude~7°~0′ and latitude~19°~30′ (1675) [1697], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~21°~40′ and latitude~7°~0′ (1676) [1698].

A spring comes out of the mountain of Ghāna; its mouth is in the sea; the beginning of the spring is at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~11/51°~40′ (1677) [1667]; it passes to longitude~39°~30′ and latitude~16/56°~40′ (1678) [1668]; then it passes by the great city of \textarabic{جرمى} to the mountain \textarabic{سرعاطوس}, cutting across it, at longitude~31°~0′ (1679) [1669]; it passes by the yellow mountain and cuts across it at longitude~31°~0′ (1680) [1670]; and passes to Qayrawān, touching it, at longitude~31°~0′, and flows into the sea at longitude~31°~30′ and latitude~32°~40′ (1681) [1671].

And there falls into this river a river from two springs called the Lakes of the Tortoises, one of them at longitude~45°~0′ and latitude~22°~20′ (1682) [1672] and the second at longitude~46°~20′ and latitude~22°~0′ (1683) [1673] out of each comes a river, and they meet at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~21°~0′ (1684) [1674] and become one river, which flows into the first at longitude~39°~40′ and latitude~16°~40′ (1685) [1675].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{باليس} by Yemen at longitude~67°~20′ and latitude~14°~0′ (1686) [1663]; it passes by Ṣanʿāʾ and by the city of \textarabic{مارا} and flows into the sea at longitude~\Ang{\langle}62°\Ang{\rangle}~50′ and latitude~16°~0′ (1687) [1664]\Pg{p. 112}.

And a river comes out of this mountain \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~67°~20′ and latitude~14°~45′ (1688) [1699]; and flows into the sea at longitude~68°~0′ and latitude~16°~40′ (1689) [1700].

A river comes out of the mountain \Ang{\langle}at\Ang{\rangle} longitude~67°~40′ and latitude~16°~0′ (1690) [1665]; it passes by Jurash and Sabaʾ and Mahra and flows into the sea at \Ang{\langle}longitude~63°~10′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~19°~0′ (1691) [1666].

A spring that does not flow at the city of \textarabic{مبس} longitude~\Ang{\langle}68°~30′ and latitude\Ang{\rangle}~18°~30′ (1692).

A spring on the first clime at longitude~\Ang{\langle}76°~40′\Ang{\rangle} (1693) [1701] a river, \textarabic{قونيسس}, comes out of it it passes by the city of \textarabic{سرٮا} and flows into the sea at longitude~75°~9′ and latitude~14°~0′ (1694) [1702].

A spring in the island of \textarabic{الميذ} at longitude~85/105°~0′ and latitude~14°~0′ (1695) [1703] two rivers come out of it: one of them passes by two cities and flows into the sea at longitude~85/105°~30′ and latitude~9°~30′ (1696) [1704] and the other passes by a city and flows into the sea at longitude~89/109°~0′ and latitude~12°~30′ (1697) [1705].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{مالا} at longitude~119°~30′ and latitude~4°~0′ (1698) [1706]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{اموزى} and passes along the coast of the sea to a small marsh at longitude~117°~30′ and latitude~6°~10′ (1699) [1707].

The river \textarabic{اروس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{مالا} also at longitude~121°~30′ and latitude~2/10/50°~45′ (1700) [1708], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~117°~40′ and latitude beyond the equator 2/10/50°~40′ (1701) [1709]\Pg{p. 113}.

The river \textarabic{مرڡس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{مالا} also at longitude~128°~30′ and latitude~2/10/50°~20′ (1702) [1710], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~125°~20′ and latitude~1°~45′ beyond the equator (1703) [1711].

The river \textarabic{حمحس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اٮا} at longitude~120°~50′ and latitude~5°~15/55′ (1704) [1712]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{ماحرولون} and flows into the sea at longitude~128°~0′ and latitude~8°~0′ (1705) [1713].

The river \textarabic{ڡاسس} from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~121°~0′ and latitude~8°~0′ (1706) [1714]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{قلمادى} and flows into the sea at longitude~121°~10′ and latitude~11°~0′ (1707) [1715].

A spring in the island of \textarabic{العراة} at longitude~136°~5′ and latitude~11°~40′ (1708) [1716]; it passes to longitude~136°~5′ and latitude~18°~0′ (1709) [1717]; then two rivers come out of here both flow into the sea one of them at longitude~137°~30′ and latitude~11°~10′ (1710) [1718] and the mouth of the other at longitude~138°~0′ and latitude~14°~30′ (1711) [1719].

A spring in the island of \textarabic{الزنج}, its beginning is at longitude~138°~0′ and latitude~4°~30′ (1712) [1720]; then flows into the sea at longitude~138°~0′ and latitude~1°~0′ (1713) [1721] and a river comes out of this river at longitude~138°~0′ and latitude~2/10/50°~0′ (1714) [1722], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~136°~0′ and latitude~2/10/50°~0′ (1715) [1723].

\Ang{\langle}The river\Ang{\rangle} \textarabic{بيسوعس} comes out of a spring in the mountain \textarabic{منيدروس}, its beginning is at longitude~147°~40′ and latitude~16°~50′ (1716) [1724]; and flows \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the sea at longitude~147°~0′ and latitude~11°~30′ (1717) [1725].

\Pg{p. 114}The river \textarabic{سالس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{منيدروس} at longitude~148°~0′ and latitude~16°~30′ (1718) [1726], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~160°~20′ and latitude~8°~10′ (1719) [1727].

A river, \textarabic{سورس}, comes out of this river and enters the island of \textarabic{الذهبانيّة}; its beginning is at longitude~150°~0′ and latitude~10°~0′ (1720) [1728], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~147°~0′ and latitude~1°~15/55′ (1721) [1729].

Out of this river \textarabic{سورس} come two rivers, one of them \textarabic{حوسان}; its beginning is at longitude~149°~0′ and latitude~7°~0′ (1722) [1730] and its end is in the sea at longitude~145°~30′ and latitude~5°~20′ (1723) [1731].

And the second river \textarabic{اطانس}, its beginning is at \Ang{\langle}longitude~148°~30′ and latitude~4°~30′ (1724) [1732], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude\Ang{\rangle}~101°~30′ and latitude~1°~45′ (1725) [1733].

The river \textarabic{سوا س} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اروس احروسس}, which is the Mountain of the Ruby; its beginning is at longitude~169°~30′ and latitude~5°~0′ (1726) [1734]; this river passes by the city of \textarabic{سوسه}, by a city without a name near the equator, by the city of \textarabic{قطيغورا} and by a city beyond the equator whose latitude is 6°~45′ (1726/1) [1735]; and flows into the sea at longitude~167°~0′ and latitude beyond the equator 9°~0′ (1727) [1736].

A river comes out of this river and turns back into it; its beginning is at longitude~166°~0′ and latitude~1°~40′ beyond the equator (1728) [1737]; it reaches longitude~166°~40′ and latitude~8°~0′ beyond the equator (1729) [1738].

And its mouth is at longitude~165°~40′ and latitude~5°~0′ beyond the equator (1730) [1739].

\Pg{p. 115}The river \textarabic{اوطاسى} comes out of a spring in the Island of the Jewels; the beginning of the spring is at longitude~174°~0′ and latitude~5°~45′ (1731) [1740]; this river passes from it to the city of \textarabic{صلمى}, then returns to the city of \textarabic{صلاى}, then passes to a marsh between the mountain and the city of \textarabic{مخلاى}; the beginning of the marsh is at latitude~5°~10′ (1732) and its end at latitude~4°~10′ (1733).

\par\medskip\begin{center}\bfseries The second clime and the springs and the rivers that are in it\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The second clime and the springs and the rivers that are in }{F121-L04}\par

The river \textarabic{رودس} comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~15°~0′ and latitude~18°~20′ (1734) [1741], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~9°~20′ and latitude~18°~20′ (1735) [1742].

The river \textarabic{ڡالوس}, which is the River of the Snakes, comes out \Ang{\langle}of the mountain\Ang{\rangle}; its beginning is at longitude~15°~0′ and latitude~20°~25′ (1736) [1743], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~\Ang{\langle}9°~40′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~20°~25′ (1737) [1744].

The river \textarabic{حوسوس} comes out of a mountain at longitude~\Ang{\langle}15°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~21°~45′ (1738) [1745], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~9°~45′ and latitude~21°~45′ (1739) [1746].

The river \textarabic{سالس} comes out of a mountain at longitude~15°~0′ and latitude~22°~25′ (1740) [1747], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~9°~0′ and latitude~28°~45′ (1741) [1748].

A river comes out of a mountain at longitude~28°~10′ and latitude~22°~30′ (1742) [1749], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~9°~0′ and latitude~25°~40′ (1743) [1750]\Pg{p. 116}.

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{سرجيس}, its beginning is at longitude~39°~20′ and latitude~21°~0′ (1744) [1751]; it passes by the mountain \textarabic{رحوما} and cuts it in two halves and flows into the sea at the city of \textarabic{ماون} at longitude~39°~40′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1745) [1752].

A river also comes out of the mountain \textarabic{سرحس}, whose mouth is in this river; its beginning is at longitude~41°~45′ and latitude~21°~0′ (1746) [1753], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~40°~0′ and latitude~24°~0′ (1747) [1754].

A spring at longitude~41°~30′ and latitude~16°~30′ (1748) [1755]; a river comes out of it to the lake \textarabic{نوبا} at longitude~44°~0′ and latitude~14°~40′ (1749) [1756]; the lake is of the size of half a degree.

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~69°~40′ and latitude~18°~30′ (1750) [1757]; it passes \textarabic{بٮاله} and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~68°~10′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1751) [1758].

The river \textarabic{لادو} comes out of a spring, its beginning is at longitude~74°~0′ and latitude~18°~20′ (1752) [1759], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~78°~20′ and latitude~22°~20′ (1753) [1760].

A river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~79°~45′ and latitude~20°~30′ (1754), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~80°~0′ and latitude~18°~0′ (1755).

A river from the mountain of Kirmān, its beginning is at longitude~94°~30′ and latitude~28°~40′ (1756) [1761], and its mouth is in the sea beside the city of \textarabic{النيرون} at longitude~92°~20′ and latitude~28°~40′ (1757) [1762].

A river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~102/110/150°~0′ and latitude~22°~0′ (1758) [1763], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~100°~0′ and latitude~20°~15′ (1759) [1764].

And a river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~102°~0′ and latitude~21°~15/55′ (1760) [1765], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~100°~30′ and latitude~20°~30′ (1761) [1766]\Pg{p. 117}.

The river \textarabic{قيدس} from a spring at the edge of the mountain \textarabic{ٮٮطعں}, its beginning is at longitude~118°~30′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1762) [1767], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~111°~30′ and latitude~14°~30′ (1763) [1768].

The river \textarabic{ڡرٮدس} from the mountain \textarabic{ٮٮطعں}, its beginning is at longitude~120°~30′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}20°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (1764) [1769], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~114°~30′ and latitude~15°~20′ (1765) [1770].

The river \textarabic{سالس} from a spring \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the mountain \textarabic{ٮٮطعں}, its beginning is at longitude~120°~20′ and latitude~19°~40′ (1766) [1771], and its mouth is \Ang{\langle}in the sea at\Ang{\rangle} longitude \textarabic{و\AngOpen{}ٮط\AngClose{}} \Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} \Ang{\langle}and latitude\Ang{\rangle}~14°~45′ (1767) [1772].

The river \textarabic{حاٮرس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اداسرون}, its beginning is at \Ang{\langle}longitude~127°~10′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~21°~10′ (1768) [1773]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{حاٮرن} and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~128°~30′ and latitude~15°~40′ (1769) [1774].

A spring from the mountain \textarabic{ادورا} a river comes out of it called \textarabic{ٮوس}, its beginning is at longitude~128°~0′ and latitude~16°~30′ (1770) [1775], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~126°~0′ and latitude~14°~40′ (1771) [1776].

A river from the mountain \textarabic{اذورا} also, its beginning is at longitude~129°~40′ and latitude~16°~20′ (1772) [1777], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~128°~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~12°~20′ (1773) [1778].

And a river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~131°~30′ and latitude~16°~30′ (1774) [1779], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~131°~0′ and latitude~14°~20′ (1775) [1780]\Pg{p. 118}.

And the river \textarabic{سرون} from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~138°~45′ and latitude~28°~40′ (1778) [1783], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~138°~40′ and latitude~17°~0′ (1779) [1784].

And the river \textarabic{دالس} also from this mountain, its beginning is at longitude~135°~15′ and latitude~24°~0′ (1780) [1785], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~134°~40′ and latitude~17°~45′ (1781) [1786].

And a river comes out of the city of \textarabic{اطراسن}, its beginning is at longitude~142°~0′ and latitude~22°~0′ (1782) [1787], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~141°~30′ and latitude~16°~30′ (1783) [1788].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{منيدروس}, its beginning is at longitude~144°~0′ and latitude~19°~0′ (1784) [1789], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~148°~0′ and latitude~15°~40′ (1785) [1790].

And a river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~145°~45′ and latitude~18°~0′ (1786), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~144°~0′ and latitude~12°~0′ (1787).

The river \textarabic{طماس} from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~147°~0′ and latitude~17°~0′ (1788) [1791], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~145°~20′ and latitude~10°~45′ (1789) [1792].

A spring in the Shining Fortress in the Dark Sea, its beginning is at longitude~176°~0′ and latitude~22°~45′ (1790) [1793] \Ang{\langle}comes out\Ang{\rangle} from it two rivers both flow into the sea the mouth of the first at longitude~178°~0′ and latitude~28°~45′ (1791) [1794] \Ang{\langle}and the mouth of the other at\Ang{\rangle} longitude~174°~0′ and latitude~22°~40′ (1792) [1795].

\par\medskip\begin{center}\Pg{p. 119}\bfseries \Ang{\langle}The\Ang{\rangle} third clime and the springs and \Ang{\langle}the rivers\Ang{\rangle} that are in it\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{AnglangleTheAngrangle third clime and the springs and Anglan}{F125-L01}\par

\Ang{\langle}A spring, its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~10°~40′ and latitude~29°~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (1793) [1796] \Ang{\langle}and its mouth is in the sea\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~8°~20′ and latitude~28°~15/55′ (1794) [1797].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}11°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}15′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~28°~0′ (1795) [1798], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~8°~0′ and latitude~27°~30′ (1796) [1799].

A spring \Ang{\langle}, its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~11°~15′ and latitude~27°~20′ (1797) [1800], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~8°~0′ and latitude~27°~0′ (1798) [1801].

The river \textarabic{حلٮڡاث}, its beginning is at longitude~18°~0′ and latitude~30°~0′ (1799) [1802], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~17°~45′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1800) [1803].

The river \textarabic{ساوس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~19°~40′ and latitude~30°~0′ (1801) [1804], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~20°~20′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1802) [1805].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~22°~45′ and latitude~29°~30′ (1803) [1808], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~23°~45′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1804) [1809].

The river \textarabic{سوسولس}, its beginning is at longitude~23°~30′ and latitude~29°~40′ (1805) [1810], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~24°~40′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1806) [1811].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~23°~40′ and latitude~25°~40′ (1807) [1812], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~24°~30′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1808) [1813].

\Pg{p. 120}A river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~37°~20′ and latitude~26°~45′ (1809) [1814], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~37°~30′ and latitude~32°~10′ (1810) [1815].

Two rivers flow into this river from two springs one of them, its beginning is at longitude~36°~30′ and latitude~29°~40′ (1811) [1816], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~37°~20′ and latitude~29°~40′ (1812) [1817] and the second, its beginning is at longitude~37°~0′ and latitude~30°~40′ (1813) [1818], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~37°~20′ and latitude~30°~0′ (1814) [1819].

A river comes out of the city of \textarabic{طاووس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~50°~0′ and latitude~28°~0′ (1815) [1820]; it passes by the mountain \textarabic{اسلوس} at latitude~29°~30′ and longitude~48°~0′ (1816) [1821]; and passes to the mountain \textarabic{مقلٮان} at latitude~27°~0′ and longitude~46°~0′ (1817) [1822]; and passes to the Mountain of the Sun at latitude~30°~0′ and longitude~48°~0′ (1818) [1823]; and flows into the sea between Barqa and \textarabic{اسفارٮوس}, at longitude~42°~20′ and latitude~38°~20′ (1819) [1824].

And a river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{زارس}, its beginning is at longitude~45°~10′ and latitude~24°~45′ (1820) [1825]; and passes \Ang{\langle}to\Ang{\rangle} latitude~28°~30′ (1821) [1826]; and flows into a small marsh at the city of \textarabic{ٮرٮاريا} at longitude~50°~0′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}24°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} (1822) [1827].

\Ang{\langle}A river from the mountain\Ang{\rangle} Kirmān, its beginning is at longitude~90°~0′ and latitude~29°~0′ (1823) [1828], and its mouth is in \Ang{\langle}the sea at longitude~89°~0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~28°~0′ (1824) [1829]\Pg{p. 121}.

And a river from this mountain also \Ang{\langle}, its beginning is at longitude~92°~0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~\Ang{\langle}27°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (1825) [1830] \Ang{\langle}and its mouth is\Ang{\rangle} in the sea at longitude~90°~0′ and latitude~26°~50′ (1826) [1831].

A river from the mountain of Kirmān also, its beginning is at longitude~93°~0′ and latitude~25°~45′ (1827) [1832], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~91°~10′ and latitude~25°~30′ (1828) [1833].

And a river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~98°~20′ and latitude~25°~0′ (1829) [1834], and its mouth is \Ang{\langle}in the sea at longitude~91°~50′ and latitude\Ang{\rangle}~24°~40′ (1830) [1835].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~100°~20′ and latitude~24°~30′ (1831) [1836], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~99°~45′ and latitude~21°~10′ (1832) [1837].

A river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~108°~30′ and latitude~25°~0′ (1833) [1838], and its mouth is in the sea at the city of \textarabic{امرس} at longitude~108°~0′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1834) [1839] falls into this river a river from the mountain \textarabic{ارٮٮطا}, its beginning is at longitude~106°~0′ and latitude~24°~30′ (1835) [1840], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~103°~30′ and latitude~20°~40′ (1836) [1841].

A river from the mountain \textarabic{اوٮدٮون}, its beginning is at longitude~121°~30′ and latitude~27°~0′ (1837) [1842]; it passes to longitude~108°~0′ and latitude~19°~30′ (1838) [1843]; then two rivers branch off from it both flow to the sea the mouth of one of them at longitude~106°~30′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1839) [1844] and the mouth of the other at longitude~106°~0′ and latitude~17°~0′ (1840) [1845].

\Pg{p. 122}A river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~125°~30′ and latitude~26°~45′ (1841) [1846]; it passes between the city of \textarabic{ڡاڡورا} and the mountain \textarabic{سنودٮس} and passes by the city of \textarabic{ايشنا} and comes to longitude~108°~30′ and latitude~16°~40′ (1842) [1847]; then two rivers branch off from it whose mouths are in the sea the mouth of one of them at longitude~106°~30′ and latitude~16°~0′ (1843) [1848] and the mouth of the other at longitude~108°~0′ and latitude~15°~0′ (1844) [1849].

The river \textarabic{دوانس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{رماسا}, its beginning is at longitude~149°~50′ and latitude~29°~0′ (1845) [1850]; then it passes to longitude~148°~30′ and latitude~27°~0′ (1846) [1851]; then it passes near the city of \textarabic{طعولا} and by the city of \textarabic{فرداثرا} and flows into the sea at longitude~153°~30′ and latitude~11°~15′ (1847) [1852].

And the river \textarabic{دورٮوس} comes out of this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~150°~45′ and latitude~29°~0′ (1848) [1853], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~155°~20′ and latitude~18°~40′ (1849) [1854].

The river \textarabic{سمس ڡس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{سمس}, its beginning is at longitude~168°~40′ and latitude~26°~30′ (1850) [1855], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~89/109°~40′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}18°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} (1851) [1856].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~10°~45′ and latitude~32°~30′ (1852) [1857]; it passes along the mountain \textarabic{دٮلوں} and flows into the sea at longitude~7°~40′ and latitude~38°~30′ (1853) [1858]\Pg{p. 123}.

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~11°~0′ and latitude~31°~30′ (1854) [1859]; it passes between the Mountain of the Sun and the mountain of the Little Atlas; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~8°~20′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1855) [1860].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~11°~15′ and latitude~31°~30′ (1856) [1861], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~12°~0′ and latitude~38°~30′ (1857) [1862].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~18°~10′ and latitude~31°~50′ (1858) [1863], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~18°~30′ and latitude~38°~0′ (1859) [1864].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~14°~40′ and latitude~31°~0′ (1862) [1867]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~14°~40′ and latitude~32°~20′ (1863) [1868].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~18°~50′ and latitude~30°~30′ (1864) [1869]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~18°~45′ and latitude~32°~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (1865) [1870].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~19°~10′ and latitude~31°~0′ (1866) [1871]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~19°~25′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1867) [1872].

A river in an island, its beginning is at longitude~30°~0′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1868) [1873]; it passes to latitude~36°~0′ along the same longitude (1869) [1874] and turns aside to the middle of the city of \textarabic{اثلا} and flows into the sea at longitude~28°~20′ and latitude~35°~40′ (1870) [1875].

A spring in this island also at longitude~30°~40′ and latitude~35°~30′ (1871) [1876] and its end is in the city of \textarabic{قا} at longitude~31°~45′ and latitude~35°~40′ (1872) [1877].

A spring in the mountain \textarabic{ٮويرٮورا}; two rivers come out of it to two small marshes; the first of them begins at longitude~33°~50′ and latitude~30°~0′ (1873) [1880]; one of the two marshes is at longitude~35°~0′ and latitude~31°~0′ (1874) [1878]\Pg{p. 124} and the second at longitude~35°~30′ and latitude~30°~40′ (1875) [1879].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~36°~0′ and latitude~30°~40′ (1876) [1881], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~35°~20′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}32°\Ang{\rangle}~0′ (1877) [1882].

A spring in an island, its beginning is at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~35°~40′ (1878) [1887], and its mouth is \Ang{\langle}in the sea at\Ang{\rangle} longitude~45°~50′ and latitude~36°~45′ (1879) [1888].

A spring in this island also, its beginning is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}45°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~35°~20′ (1880) [1883]; its mouth is in the sea at\footnoteT{The print has \textarabic{غند}, with a dot over the ʿayn, for \textarabic{عند}, «at».} longitude~46°~20′ and latitude~36°~0′ (1881) [1884].

And a spring in this island also, its beginning is at longitude~45°~45′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1882) [1885], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~46°~30′ and latitude~35°~20′ (1883) [1886].

A spring in an island, its beginning is at longitude~47°~0′ and latitude~34°~0′ (1884) [1889], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~47°~0′ and latitude~34°~30′ (1885) [1890].

And a spring in this island also, its beginning is at longitude~47°~30′ and latitude~34°~0′ (1886), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~47°~0′ and latitude~34°~30′ (1887).

And a spring in this island also, its beginning is at longitude~48°~0′ and latitude~33°~40′ (1888) [1891], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~47°~20′ and latitude~34°~0′ (1889) [1892].

And a spring its size is half a degree from which no river comes near Alexandria at longitude~50°~30′ and latitude~30°~30′ (1890) [1893].

A spring in the island of Cyprus, its beginning is at longitude~56°~10′ and latitude~34°~40′ (1891) [1894], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~55°~50′ and latitude~35°~20′ (1892) [1895].

A spring in this island also, its beginning is in the city of \textarabic{سمار} at longitude~57°~40′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1893) [1896]\Pg{p. 125}, and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~57°~0′ and latitude~34°~20′ (1894) [1897].

The Lake of Tiberias, of the size of half a degree, its length like its width, at longitude~58°~20′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1895) [1025]; a river comes out of it and passes by the Mountain of Snow and the mountain \textarabic{سنير}, then flows into the sea at longitude~61°~0′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1896) [1557]; and there also comes out of this lake a river to the Dead Lake, at longitude~58°~20′ and latitude~30°~30′ (1897) [1026]; the size of the Dead Lake is half a degree, round.

A river from the Mountain of Snow, its beginning is at longitude~59°~45′ and latitude~32°~15′ (1898), and its mouth is in the city of Damascus: it passes a little beyond it to a lake into which it flows.

A river comes out of Mount Lebanon, its beginning is at longitude~61°~0′ and latitude~33°~40′ (1899) [1898]; and passes by Ḥimṣ and Ḥamāh and it is the Jordan and flows into the sea at Antioch at longitude~61°~0′ and latitude~34°~45′ (1900) [1899].

A river comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~79°~20′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}35°\Ang{\rangle}~40′ (1901) [1900]; and passes and cuts across the mountain of Iṣṭakhr and Jūr and flows into the sea near Sīrāf at longitude~80°~0′ and latitude~29°~\Ang{\langle}30′\Ang{\rangle} (1902) [1901].

A river comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~84°~30′ and latitude~35°~40′ (1903) [1902]; and flows into the sea at longitude~84°~40′ and latitude~29°~20′ (1904) [1903].

The river \textarabic{روس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{سيمس}, its beginning is at longitude~158°~30′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1905) [1904], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~158°~0′ and latitude~18/58°~50′ (1906) [1905].

The river \textarabic{ڡطروس}: its beginning is a spring from the mountain of the river \textarabic{سمس}, at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~166°~10′ and latitude~31°~30′ (1907) [1906]\Pg{p. 126}; it passes to a marsh the size of a degree at longitude~165°~20′ and latitude~20°~40′ (1908) [1907]; and passes from this marsh and cuts through the city of China, and passes on until it flows into the sea at longitude~162°~10′ and latitude~3°~40′ (1909) [1908]. And there falls into the marsh that is on this river a river from the mountain of the river \textarabic{سمس} also; \Ang{\langle}its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~169°~0′ and latitude~28°~0′ (1910) [1909], and its mouth is in the marsh.

\par\medskip\begin{center}\bfseries The fifth clime and the springs and the rivers that are in it\end{center}\pdfbookmark[1]{The fifth clime and the springs and the rivers that are in i}{F132-L06}\par

The river \textarabic{اس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~12°~15/55′ and latitude~40°~45′ (1911) [1910], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~4°~30′ and latitude~38°~20′ (1912) [1911]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{ڡسطورن} and by the city of \textarabic{اسقلس} and by the city of \textarabic{اروثى}.

A river from the mountain \textarabic{ارطسڡازا}, its beginning is at longitude~12°~15′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~38°~0′\Ang{\rangle} (1913) [1912]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{وٮه} and between the mountain \textarabic{لوس} and the city of \textarabic{اطلما} at longitude~7°~40′ and latitude~38°~40′ (1914) [1913]; then two rivers branch off from it both flow into the sea the mouth of the first at longitude~7°~0′ and latitude~37°~20′ (1915) [1914] and the other at longitude~6°~30′ and latitude~37°~20′ (1916) [1915]; this river goes round the mountain \textarabic{لوس} and the city of \textarabic{اووى} until it flows into the sea.

A river comes out of the city of \textarabic{مارٮا} and passes by the city of \textarabic{مكسارا}, its beginning is at longitude~14°~30′ and latitude~40°~40′ (1917) [1917], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~18°~0′ and latitude~45°~40′ (1918) [1918]\Pg{p. 127}.

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{ايدوقرا}, its beginning is at longitude~15°~35′ and latitude~39°~40′ (1919) [1919], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~16°~20′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}38°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} (1920) [1920].

A river in the island of \textarabic{ڡرس}, its beginning is at longitude~30°~20′ and latitude~39°~40′ (1921) [1921]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{شرفى} and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~29°~25′ and latitude~40°~15′ (1922) [1922].

A river in the island of Sarakhs, its beginning is a spring at longitude~30°~20′ and latitude~36°~50′ (1923) [1923], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~28°~40′ and latitude~37°~0′ (1924) [1924].

And a river in this island also, its beginning is a spring at longitude~31°~0′ and latitude~36°~0′ (1925) [1925]; it passes inside the city of \textarabic{لٮيس} and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~32°~0′ and latitude~37°~0′ (1926) [1926].

A spring in the island of \textarabic{قرنس}, its beginning is at longitude~31°~45′ and latitude~39°~15/55′ (1927) [1927]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 31°~10′ and latitude~40°~20′ (1928) [1928].

Then two rivers branch off from this place; one of them enters the city of \textarabic{قروى} and comes out from it and flows into the sea at longitude~32°~0′ and latitude~39°~10′ (1929) [1929] and the other river flows into the sea at longitude~31°~30′ and latitude~40°~45′ (1930) [1930].

And a river also comes out of this spring, its beginning is at longitude~31°~30′ and latitude~40°~10′ (1931) [1931], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~32°~15/55′ and latitude~40°~10′ (1932) [1932].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اطى} in an island, its beginning is at longitude~35°~0′ and latitude~36°~15′ (1933) [1933]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~38°~40′ and latitude~36°~45′ (1934) [1934]\Pg{p. 128}.

And a river in this island also, its beginning is a spring at longitude~35°~25′ and latitude~37°~10′ (1935) [1935], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~35°~45′ and latitude~35°~30′ (1936) [1936].

A river comes out of a city; it has two mouths into the sea, at the two sides of the city; the beginning of the river is at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~40°~45′ (1937) [1937] its first mouth is at longitude~40°~40′ and latitude~42°~20′ (1938) [1938] and the second mouth at longitude~45°~0′ and latitude~40°~0′ (1939) [1939].

A river comes out of the city of, its beginning is at longitude~46°~30′ and latitude~37°~0′ (1940) [1940], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~47°~10′ and latitude~36°~30′ (1941) [1941].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~52°~0′ and latitude~40°~30′ (1942) [1942]; it passes to the city of \textarabic{طروب}; its mouth is in the city, at longitude~49°~40′ and latitude~40°~30′ (1943) [1943].

A spring in the city of \textarabic{قرغامس}; three rivers come out of it.

The first of the rivers: \Ang{\langle}its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~50°~30′ and latitude~40°~20′ (1944) [1944]; it passes by the mountain \textarabic{اندس} and cuts it and flows into the sea at longitude~49°~50′ and latitude~41°~30′ (1945) [1945].

And the second river, its beginning is at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~40°~30′ (1946) [1946]; it passes and cuts through the mountain \textarabic{اندس}, passes inside the city of \textarabic{ابليون}, comes out of it and flows into the sea at longitude~50°~40′ and latitude~42°~45′ (1947) [1947].

And the third river, its beginning is at longitude~51°~40′ and latitude~40°~30′ (1948) [1948]; it passes touching the beginning of the boundary of the mountain \textarabic{اندس} and enters the city of \textarabic{اٮلون} and comes out from it and flows into the sea at longitude~50°~50′ and latitude~48°~10′ (1949) [1949].

A river comes out of the city of \textarabic{افاسس} at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~37°~40′ (1950) [1676 1950]\Pg{p. 129} it passes between the mountain \textarabic{سيفولس} and the mountain \textarabic{ٮلمون} it passes to the city of \textarabic{قبلوميلس} and turns back and passes between the mountain \textarabic{سيفولس} and the mountain \textarabic{درومر} and passes to a place whose longitude is 15°~0′ and latitude~45°~0′ (1951) [1677 1951] and turns back, touching the city of \textarabic{اطوما}, between it and \textarabic{هرقله}, and flows into the sea at longitude~12°~40′ and latitude~47°~0′ (1952) [1678 1952].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~56°~0′ and latitude~40°~0′ (1953) [1679 1953] it passes \textarabic{باٮقره} and flows into the sea at longitude~56°~25′ and latitude~49°~20′ (1954) [1680 1954].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~56°~15′ and latitude~37°~30′ (1955) [1955], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~56°~20′ and latitude~35°~35′ (1956) [1956].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~57°~0′ and latitude~40°~20′ (1957) [1957]; it passes between the city of \textarabic{اماسيا} and the city of \textarabic{سيسماى} and passes at the corner of the city of \textarabic{تيمنا} and flows into the sea at longitude~\Ang{\langle}59°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}25′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~48°~40′ (1958) [1958].

The river Tigris: its beginning is a spring at longitude~64°~40′ and latitude~39°~0′ (1959) [1483]; it passes between two mountains at longitude~64°~45′ and latitude~37°~45′ (1960) [1484] and turns aside along the mountain to longitude~68°~0′ and latitude~38°~0′ (1961) [1485]\Pg{p. 130}; then it passes to Āmid, then to Balad; between Balad and Shahrazūr it passes between Mosul and the mountain also; it passes by Takrīt, passes by Surra man raʾā, enters Baghdad \Ang{\langle}and comes out of\Ang{\rangle} it, passes by al-Madāʾin and passes by Wā\Ang{\langle}siṭ\Ang{\rangle} on the side of Ḥulwān; then it comes to the marshes.

\textarabic{م\AngOpen{}. . .\AngClose{}} the marshes, the size of a degree; the middle of the marshes is at longitude~78°~0′ and latitude~32°~0′ (1962) [1028 1486].

And it passes to longitude~74°~0′ and latitude~31°~45′ (1963), and from there it divides into three rivers.

The first of them has its mouth at Basra; the second passes on, its mouth between Basra, ʿAbbādān and al-Ahwāz; and the third flows into the sea at longitude~75°~45′ and latitude~30°~30′ (1964).

And there falls into the Tigris a river called \textarabic{ذيب}, from a mountain; its beginning is at longitude~68°~30′ and latitude~39°~0′ (1965) [1495], and its mouth is in the Tigris at longitude~68°~20′ and latitude~37°~30′ (1966) [1496].

And there also falls into the Tigris the river \textarabic{فاقس}; its beginning is a spring in a mountain at longitude~69°~0′ and latitude~39°~20′ (1967) [1497], and its mouth is in the Tigris at longitude~69°~15′ and latitude~36°~30′ (1968) [1498].

And there fall into the Tigris the two Zābs, from a mountain: the beginning of one of them is at longitude~71°~30′ and latitude~38°~30′ (1969) [1499], and its mouth at longitude~69°~30′ and latitude~35°~30′ (1970) [1500]; the second Zāb begins at longitude~72°~0′ and latitude~38°~0′ (1971) [1501], and its mouth is at longitude~69°~40′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1972) [1502].

A spring at the foot of the mountain \textarabic{حارث وحويرث}, which does not flow, at longitude~69°~10′ and latitude~40°~20′ (1973) [1959]; its size is two quarters of a degree.

A river comes out of a mountain; its beginning is at longitude~87°~0′ and latitude~39°~0′ (1974) [1960]; it passes and cuts through Marw al-Rūdh, comes out of it and comes to Marw at longitude~83°~45′ and latitude~37°~45′ (1975) [1961]; then it vanishes.

A marsh of the size of half a degree; its middle is at longitude~86°~0′ and latitude~36°~40′ (1976) [1027]. Two springs flow into it from a mountain, one of them at longitude~80°~45′ and latitude~38°~30′ (1977) [1962] and the second at longitude~89°~20′ and latitude~39°~10′ (1978) [1963].

\Pg{p. 131}The river Mihrān, its beginning is at longitude~126°~30′ and latitude~36°~10′ (1979) [1613]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 125°~20′ and latitude~32°~20′ (1980) [1614]; then it passes to a place whose longitude is 122°~30′ and latitude~32°~45′ (1981) [1615]; then it passes to a place whose longitude is 120°~10′ and latitude~32°~40′ (1982) [1616]; then it passes to a place whose longitude is \Ang{\langle}119°\Ang{\rangle}~0′ and latitude~31°~0′ (1983) [1617]; then it passes between the yellow mountain and a city at longitude~111°~15′ and latitude~26°~0′ (1984) [1618]; then it passes to a place whose longitude is 107°~0′ and latitude~23°~30′ (1985) [1619].

Then two rivers branch off from this place; the mouth of the first of them in the sea at the city of \textarabic{اميرس} at longitude~104°~15′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1986) [1620].

Then a river comes out of this river from a place whose longitude is 106°~30′ and latitude~28°~30′ (1987) [1621]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 106°~0′ and latitude~22°~10′ (1988) [1622].

Two rivers branch off from this place; the mouth of the first of them in the sea at longitude~104°~45′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1989) [1623] and the mouth of the second in the sea at longitude~105°~30′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1990) [1624].

The second, great river Mihrān: its mouth is in the sea at longitude~106°~30′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1991) [1625].

Two rivers also come out of the river; the beginning of one of them at longitude~107°~0′ and latitude~22°~20′ (1992) [1626], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~106°~0′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1993) [1627] and the beginning of the second river at longitude~106°~40′ and latitude~21°~0′ (1994) [1628], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~106°~15′ and latitude~20°~0′ (1995) [1629].

A spring falls to the river Mihrān, its beginning is at longitude~125°~30′ and latitude~36°~30′ (1996)\Pg{p. 132}, and its mouth is in the river at longitude~125°~40′ and latitude~35°~0′ (1997).

And a spring falls also to the river Mihrān, its beginning is at longitude~124°~40′ and latitude~37°~0′ (1998) [1630], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~122°~30′ and latitude~32°~45′ (1999) [1631].

And a spring also falls to the river Mihrān, its beginning is at longitude~123°~0′ and latitude~36°~0′ (2000) [1632], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~120°~0′ and latitude~32°~45′ (2001) [1633].

And a spring falls also to the river Mihrān, its beginning is at longitude~83/103°~40′ and latitude~36°~0′ (2002) [1634], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~120°~10′ and latitude~32°~10′ (2003) [1635].

And there fall into this spring three rivers from three springs: the beginning of the first spring is at longitude~115°~0′ and latitude~36°~0′ (2004) [1636], and its mouth is in the river of the spring at longitude~116°~40′ and latitude~32°~30′ (2005) [1637]; the beginning of the second spring is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}106°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~36°~0′ (2006) [1638], and its mouth is in the river of the spring at longitude~108°~0′ and latitude~32°~30′ (2007) [1639]; and the third spring begins at longitude~108°~0′ and latitude~36°~10′ (2008) [1640], and its mouth is in the river of the spring at longitude~109°~30′ and latitude~32°~30′ (2009) [1641].

A river comes out between the city of Eastern Alexandria and the mountain at longitude~104°~40′ and latitude~32°~50′ (2010) [1642]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 103°~0′ and latitude~32°~0′ (2011) [1643]\Pg{p. 133}; and passes between the city of Alexandria and the mountain and passes near the city of Qandahār and passes to a place whose longitude is 114°~0′ and latitude~29°~25′ (2012) [1644]; and passes and falls into the river Mihrān the first that we have mentioned at longitude~119°~0′ and latitude~30°~30′ (2013) [1645].

Out of the river of this spring there comes a river that is also called the river Mihrān; its beginning is at longitude~88°~30′ and latitude~29°~30′ (2014) [1646]; it passes near Qandahār and passes and touches the mountain of Kirmān, and flows into the sea near al-Manṣūra at longitude~92°~20′ and latitude~22°~50′ (2015) [1647].

There falls into this river Mihrān a river from a mountain; its beginning is at longitude~83/103°~20′ and latitude~28°~20′ (2016) [1648]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 84/104°~40′ and latitude~27°~30′ (2017) [1649], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~88/108°~15′ and latitude~26°~40′ (2018) [1650].

And there also falls into this river Mihrān a river from the mountain \textarabic{ارسطا}; its beginning is at longitude~107°~30′ and latitude~25°~0′ (2019) [1651], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~109°~40′ and latitude~24°~30′ (2020) [1652].

The river Ganges, its beginning is a spring from a mountain at longitude~135°~0′ and latitude~39°~0′ (2021) [1964]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 135°~30′ and latitude~31°~0′ (2022); and passes to a place whose longitude is 139°~30′ and latitude~28°~0′ (2023) [1965]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 140°~10′ and latitude~26°~30′ (2024) [1966]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 139°~0′ and latitude~22°~0′ (2025) [1967]\Pg{p. 134}.

Two rivers come out of this place; the first of them reaches a place whose longitude is 137°~0′ and latitude~21°~30′ (2026) [1968], and flows into the sea at longitude~135°~20′ and latitude~17°~45′ (2027) [1969].

A river comes out of this river, its beginning is at longitude~137°~0′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}21°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} (2028) [1970], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~137°~30′ and latitude~18°~20′ (2029) [1971].

And the second river comes out of the river Ganges, its beginning is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}139°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~22°~0′ (2030) [1972], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~141°~0′ and latitude~17°~0′ (2031) [1973].

And two rivers come out of this river: the beginning of one of them is at longitude~139°~20′ and latitude~21°~0′ (2032) [1974], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~138°~0′ and latitude~18°~10′ (2033) [1975]; and the beginning of the second is at longitude~139°~30′ and latitude~20°~0′ (2034) [1976], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~139°~0′ and latitude~18°~40′ (2035) [1977].

And there falls into the river Ganges a spring, its beginning is at longitude~134°~0′ and latitude~37°~10′ (2036) [1978], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~135°~30′ and latitude~33°~30′ (2037) [1979].

And there falls into the river Ganges also a spring from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~137°~10′ and latitude~38°~30′ (2038) [1980], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~135°~30′ and latitude~33°~30′ (2039) [1981].

And there falls into the river Ganges also a spring from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~138°~40′ and latitude~25°~0′ (2040) [1982], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~139°~30′ and latitude~28°~0′ (2041) [1983].

And there falls into the river Ganges also a river from the mountain \textarabic{سورون}, its beginning is at longitude~142°~30′ and latitude~32°~30′ (2042) [1984], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~139°~30′ and latitude~28°~0′ (2043) [1985]\Pg{p. 135}.

And there falls into the river Ganges also a river from the mountain \textarabic{سورون}, its beginning is at longitude~144°~30′ and latitude~28°~40′ (2044) [1986], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~140°~10′ and latitude~26°~30′ (2045) [1987].

The river \textarabic{باطس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~146°~0′ and latitude~39°~40′ (2046) [1653]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 149°~0′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2047) [1654]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 88/108°~0′ and latitude~40°~10′ (2048) [1655]\footnoteT{The print has \textarabic{ڡح} here and at (2053), and \textarabic{ڡو} at (2055): 88 or 108, and 86 or 106. Neither reading fits a river that runs from 149° to 180°, or tributaries that begin at 149°~30′ and 156°~30′.}; and passes and enters between the mountain of the river \textarabic{سقس} and the dam, and passes below the city of Gog and Magog, touching it, and passes to a place whose longitude is 180°~0′ and latitude~47°~30′ (2049) [1656].

And there falls into this river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~147°~20′ and latitude~45°~20′ (2050) [1657], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~149°~0′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2051) [1658].

And there also falls into the river \textarabic{ٮاطس} \Ang{\langle}a spring\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is between two cities and a mountain, at longitude~149°~30′ and latitude~38°~40′ (2052) [1659], \Ang{\langle}and its mouth is in\Ang{\rangle} the river \Ang{\langle}at\Ang{\rangle} longitude~88/108°~0′ and latitude~40°~0′ (2053) [1660].

And there also falls into the river \textarabic{ٮاطس} a river from the mountain \textarabic{اوفروس}, its beginning is at longitude~156°~30′ and latitude~39°~45′ (2054) [1661], \Ang{\langle}and its mouth is\Ang{\rangle} in the river at longitude~86/106°~25′ and latitude~41°~0′ (2055) [1662].

The river \textarabic{ٮوارٮرس}, its beginning is at longitude~12°~0′ and latitude~41°~0′ (2056) [1988], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~6°~10′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2057) [1989]\Pg{p. 136}.

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~12°~15/55′ and latitude~44°~10′ (2058) [1990], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~6°~40′ and latitude~44°~10′ (2059) [1991].

A river, its beginning is a spring at longitude~9°~10′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2060) [1992], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~7°~0′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2061) [1993].

A river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~19°~30′ and latitude~44°~20′ (2062) [1994], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~18°~0′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2063) [1995].

A river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~20°~0′ and latitude~44°~0′ (2064) [1996], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~21°~0′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2065) [1997].

A river from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~20°~15/55′ and latitude~44°~20′ (2066) [2000], and its mouth is in the sea at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~21°~30′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2067) [2001].

The river \textarabic{اغتل} from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~20°~30′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2068) [1998], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~21°~50′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2069) [1999].

A river comes out of this mountain \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is at longitude~20°~40′ and latitude~44°~50′ (2070) [2002]; it passes below the city of \textarabic{مارٮانوس}, then reaches longitude~20°~0′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2071) [2003], and flows into the sea at longitude~18°~30′ and latitude~47°~10′ (2072) [2004].

The river \textarabic{درٮوس}, its beginning is from the mountain \textarabic{اروس} at longitude~27°~40′ and latitude~44°~50′ (2073) [2005]; and passes between the city of \textarabic{لوعزٮون} and the city of \textarabic{ٮاٮوٮا}, then reaches longitude~28°~0′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2074) [2006]\Pg{p. 137}; then it turns aside and flows into the sea at longitude~22°~20′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2075) [2007].

And a river that flows into the sea comes out of this river; its beginning is at longitude~22°~20′ and latitude~43°~0′ (2076) [2008], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~22°~40′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}42°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}30′\Ang{\rangle} (2077) [2009].

And there falls \Ang{\langle}into\Ang{\rangle} the river \textarabic{دريوس} a river from the mountain \textarabic{اروس}, its beginning is at longitude~27°~15/55′ and latitude~44°~30′ (2078) [2010], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~26°~30′ and latitude~44°~30′ (2079) [2011]; and passes between the city of \textarabic{دقسالا} and the city of \textarabic{ڡلون}.

And there falls into the river \textarabic{درٮوس} also a river from the mountain \textarabic{اروس} at longitude~27°~0′ and latitude~43°~10′ (2080) [2012], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~22°~30′ and latitude~43°~30′ (2081) [2013].

And there falls into the river \textarabic{دريوس} also a river from the mountain \textarabic{اروس}, its beginning is at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~27°~0′ and latitude~42°~40′ (2082) [2014]; it passes between the city of \textarabic{طلسا} and the city of \textarabic{دقسالا} and flows into the river at longitude~22°~20′ and latitude~43°~10′ (2083) [2015].

Two rivers come out of this river, both flowing into the sea: the first of them, its beginning is at longitude~24°~40′ and latitude~48°~10′ (2084) [2016], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~24°~40′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2085) [2017]; and the second river, its beginning is at longitude~26°~10′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2086) [2018], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~26°~10′ and latitude~42°~30′ (2087) [2019].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اروس}, passes between the city of \textarabic{ڡلون} and the river \textarabic{درٮوس}, and flows into the city of \textarabic{ٮاٮونا}; its beginning is at longitude~27°~30′ and latitude~43°~40′ (2088) [2020], and its mouth is in the city at longitude~28°~40′ and latitude~44°~30′ (2089) [2021].

A river comes out of a spring, its beginning is at longitude~28°~40′ and latitude~44°~20′ (2090) [2022]\Pg{p. 138}; it passes near the city of \textarabic{منطيا} at longitude~31°~0′ and latitude~48°~40′ (2091) [2023], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~38°~30′ and latitude~44°~10′ (2092) [2024].

There falls into this river a river from a spring, its beginning is at longitude~29°~15′ and latitude~42°~45′ (2093) [2025], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~30°~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~44°~0′ (2094) [2026].

And there also falls into the first river another spring, its beginning is at longitude~29°~30′ and latitude~45°~10′ (2095) [2027], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~30°~30′ and latitude~44°~0′ (2096) [2028].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~30°~30′ and latitude~44°~45′ (2097) [2029], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~31°~20′ and latitude~44°~20′ (2098) [2030].

A spring comes out from below the city of \textarabic{نبانبا}, its beginning is at longitude~32°~15/55′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude\Ang{\rangle}~42°~35′ (2099) [2031]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~29°~30′ and latitude~41°~40′ (2100) [2032].

A spring between the city of \textarabic{ٮاٮٮدوں} and the city of \textarabic{اٮڡس}, its beginning is at longitude~33°~45′ and latitude~48°~30′ (2101) [2033], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~31°~45′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2102) [2034].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~35°~20′ and latitude~48°~45′ (2103) [1681 2035]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 35°~0′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2104) [1682 2036]; it passes on and enters the city of great Rome, comes out, and flows into the sea at longitude~35°~20′ and latitude~41°~40′ (2105) [1683 2037].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~37°~25′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2106) [2038]; it passes between the city of \textarabic{قوما} and the city of \textarabic{ڡارنبسطين} and flows into the sea at longitude~36°~25′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2107) [2039]\Pg{p. 139}.

A river comes out of a city on the sea, its beginning is at longitude~41°~35′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2108) [2040], and its mouth is in a city at longitude~48°~0′ and latitude~42°~10′ (2109) [2041].

A river comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~48°~45′ and latitude~42°~50′ (2110) [2042]; it passes between two cities that have no names and flows into the sea between a city on which there is no name on the map and the city of \textarabic{لرسو}, at longitude~45°~10′ and latitude~40°~0′ (2111) [2043].

A river comes out of a mountain and passes between a city without a name on the map and the city of \textarabic{اسلينو}; its beginning is at longitude~44°~10′ and latitude~48°~10′ (2112) [2044], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~41°~0′ (2113) [2045].

A river comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~48°~50′ (2114), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~45°~0′ and latitude~42°~20′ (2115).

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{ٮلمون}, its beginning is at longitude~54°~30′ and latitude~42°~0′ (2116) [2046], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~55°~40′ and latitude~35°~40′ (2117) [2047].

A spring from which no river flows, at longitude~58°~40′ and latitude~44°~40′ (2118); its size is a sixth of a degree.

The river \textarabic{طور}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~68°~0′ and latitude~44°~40′ (2119) [2048]; it passes between the sea and the city of Jurzān, and passes between the city of Bardhaʿa and the city of \textarabic{باسيا} and flows into the sea at longitude~74°~40′ and latitude~42°~15′ (2120) [2049].

The river Euphrates, its beginning is a spring in the mountain \textarabic{اوردخس} at longitude~68°~30′ and latitude~41°~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} (2121) [1487]\Pg{p. 140}; \Ang{\langle}it passes to the edge of\Ang{\rangle} the mountain \textarabic{مٮڡنبا} at longitude~60°~20′ and latitude~42°~20′ (2122) [1488]; and passes between Malaṭya and the city of \textarabic{هنزيط}; and passes between the mountain al-Lukkām and the city of Shimshāṭ \Ang{\langle}and the city of\Ang{\rangle} Sumaysāṭ, on its bank; and passes between a mountain \Ang{\langle}and\Ang{\rangle} the city of Manbij, and over the bridge of Manbij; and passes by Bālis, al-Raqqa and al-Rāfiqa, all of them, with what lies between them; and passes on and surrounds ʿĀna, al-Ḥadītha, Ālūsa and al-Nāʾūsa; and passes on and touches Hīt; and passes by al-Anbār; and a part of it passes to Kufa; and it passes to a place whose longitude is 71°~0′ and latitude~31°~0′ (2123) [1489], and its mouth is in the marshes at longitude~78°~0′ and latitude~31°~30′ (2124) [1490].

And a river also comes out of the Euphrates, from a place whose longitude is 71°~0′ and latitude~31°~0′ (2125) [1491], and flows into the marshes.

And there also comes out of the Euphrates \Ang{\langle}a river\Ang{\rangle} touching al-Anbār, between al-Anbār and Kufa, from a place whose longitude is 68°~50′ and latitude~32°~25′ (2126) [1494], and its mouth is in Baghdad at longitude~69°~40′ and latitude~38°~0′ (2127).

And there falls into the river Euphrates a river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~62°~20′ and latitude~41°~0′ (2128) [1492]; it passes by the city of \textarabic{هنزيط} and flows into the Euphrates at longitude~61°~30′ and latitude~39°~20′ (2129) [1493].

\Pg{p. 141}A river comes out of a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~71°~45′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2130) [2050]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 71°~50′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2131) [2051]; it passes between Bardhaʿa and the city of \textarabic{نشوى} and flows into the sea at longitude~74°~50′ and latitude~41°~45′ (2132) [2052].
