\Ang{\langle}The river\Ang{\rangle} \textarabic{اسماٮوس}, its beginning is \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the mountain \textarabic{ٮورسس} at longitude~25°~0′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2138) [2062]; \Ang{\langle}it passes by the city of\Ang{\rangle} \textarabic{درڡطٯ} and flows into the sea at longitude~21°~0′ and latitude~51°~50′ (2139) [2063].

The river \textarabic{رنوس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~28°~30′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2140) [2058], by the city of \textarabic{السٮس} and by the city of \textarabic{ٮورما}, and flows into the sea at longitude~29°~0′ and latitude~57°~0′ (2141) [2059]\Pg{p. 142}.

And there flows into this river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~26°~40′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2142) [2060], and its mouth is at longitude~28°~40′ and latitude~49°~45′ (2143) [2061].

And there also comes out of the river \textarabic{ديوس} a river, its beginning is at longitude~29°~0′ and latitude~55°~20′ (2144) [2064], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~28°~0′ and latitude~56°~20′ (2145) [2065].

The river \textarabic{نويس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~29°~20′ and latitude~46°~20′ (2146) [2066]; it passes between \textarabic{نياقيلون} and the city of \textarabic{اراطى}; it passes to a place whose longitude is 40°~40′ and latitude~47°~15′ (2147) [2067]; and passes between the city of \textarabic{ورقس} and the city of \textarabic{انردس} to a place whose longitude is 42°~0′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2148) [2068]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 44°~0′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2149) [2069]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 47°~30′ and latitude~45°~0′ (2150) [2070]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 48°~0′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2151) [2071]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 47°~30′ and latitude~48°~30′ (2152) [2072]; and passes and flows into the sea at longitude~51°~0′ and latitude~51°~40′ (2153) [2073].

And six rivers come out of this river and flow into the sea. The first of the rivers: its source is from longitude~48°~45′ and latitude~49°~50′ (2154) [2074]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~51°~0′ and latitude~49°~0′ (2155) [2075]. And the five rivers: \Ang{\langle}their mouths\Ang{\rangle} are between the mouth of this river and the mouth of the first, great river, at equal distances, in five parts (2156–2160) [2076–2080].

And there comes out of this first, great river a river, from a place whose longitude is 44°~0′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2161) [2081]\Pg{p. 143}; it passes near, below the city of \textarabic{داطه}, and passes at the corner of the city of \textarabic{دواسس}, and passes at the corner of the city of \textarabic{اكلس}, and falls into the river out of which it comes, at longitude~47°~30′ and latitude~45°~0′ (2162) [2082].

And there falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}34°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~46°~\Ang{\langle}15′\Ang{\rangle} (2163) [2083], and its mouth is in the river, touching a city \Ang{\langle}that has no\Ang{\rangle} name, at longitude~48°~0′ and latitude~46°~50′ (2164) [2084].

And there also falls into this great river a river from a city \Ang{\langle}that has no\Ang{\rangle} name on the map; \Ang{\langle}its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~\Ang{\langle}34°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~45°~45′ (2165) [2085], and its mouth is in the river, also touching a city, at longitude~48°~40′ and latitude~46°~15′ (2166) [2086].

And there falls into the great river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{سورطاٮا}, its beginning is at longitude~40°~15′ and latitude~49°~30′ (2167) [2087], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~42°~0′ and latitude~48°~0′ (2168) [2088].

And there also falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~48°~10′ (2169) [2089], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2170) [2090].

And there also falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~48°~40′ (2171), and its mouth is in the river at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2172)\Pg{p. 144}.

And there also falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~46°~10′ and latitude~48°~40′ (2173) [2091], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~45°~30′ and latitude~45°~30′ (2174) [2092].

And there also falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~46°~10′ and latitude~48°~5′ (2175) [2093], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~46°~40′ and latitude~45°~30′ (2176) [2094].

And there also falls into the great river a spring, its beginning is \Ang{\langle}at\Ang{\rangle} longitude~46°~40′ and latitude~49°~5′ (2177) [2095]; its mouth is in the river at longitude~47°~30′ and latitude~45°~20′ (2178) [2096].

And there also falls into this river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~44°~30′ and latitude~48°~40′ (2179) [2097], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~50°~30′ and latitude~51°~25′ (2180) [2098].

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~53°~30′ and latitude~45°~50′ (2181), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~58°~0′ and latitude~46°~40′ (2182).

A spring, its beginning is at longitude~54°~0′ and latitude~45°~50′ (2183), and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~58°~20′ and latitude~46°~40′ (2184).

A spring from the city of \textarabic{اماسٮ}, its beginning is at longitude~57°~20′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~45°~0′ (2185) [2099]; and flows into the sea at longitude~58°~40′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~49°~0′ (2185/1) [2100].

A spring from the city of \textarabic{زيلا}, its beginning is at longitude~59°~10′ and latitude~45°~10′ (2186) [2101]\Pg{p. 145}, and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~60°~5′ and latitude~47°~40′ (2187) [2102].

The river Jayḥān, its beginning is a spring at longitude~60°~30′ and latitude~46°~5′ (2188) [1551]; it passes at the corner of the city of \textarabic{سسماى} and passes between two mountains at longitude~\Ang{\langle}58°\Ang{\rangle}~0′ and latitude~39°~0′ (2189) [1552]; \Ang{\langle}it passes to\Ang{\rangle} the city of \textarabic{نوسا} \Ang{\langle}and Zibaṭra\Ang{\rangle}, and passes between two mountains at longitude~60°~0′ and latitude~38°~0′ (2190) [1553]; and passes by al-Maṣṣīṣa and flows into the sea between al-Maṣṣīṣa and the mountain al-Lukkām, touching al-Maṣṣīṣa, at longitude~60°~10′ and latitude~35°~40′ (2191) [1554].

A river comes out of the mountain \textarabic{قوقا}, its beginning is at longitude~72°~0′ and latitude~47°~30′ (2192), and its mouth is in a marsh, touching the city of \textarabic{طرمى} on the side next to the city of \textarabic{اوٮسه}, at longitude~69°~0′ and latitude~52°~30′ (2193).

The river \textarabic{اقسس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{قوقا}, its beginning is at longitude~72°~30′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2194), and its mouth is in the marsh, touching the city of \textarabic{طرمى} from the other side, at longitude~69°~10′ and latitude~53°~0′ (2195).

The river of Balkh, its beginning is a lake at longitude~100°~30′ and latitude~43°~0′ (2196) [1029 1559]; the size of the lake is one degree. It passes to the corner of a mountain at longitude~102°~0′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2197) [1560]; and turns back to a place whose longitude is \Ang{\langle}96°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}40′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~44°~30′ (2198) [1561]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 96°~30′ and latitude~42°~0′ (2199) [1562]; and it comes and cuts through the city of Khwārazm, comes out of it and comes to a place whose longitude is 91°~30′ and latitude~39°~40′ (2200) [1563]; and passes near Balkh and flows into a marsh at longitude~88°~0′ and latitude~39°~10′ (2201) [1564].

And this marsh extends from longitude~86°~30′ (2202) [1030] to longitude~90°~0′ (2203) [1031]\Pg{p. 146}, and in latitude between the mouth of \Ang{\langle}the river of\Ang{\rangle} Balkh and latitude~42°~0′ (2204) [1032]; and from the mouth of the river of Balkh to its edge on the side next to Khwārazm that side becomes narrow: its longitude on the side next to Khwārazm is 90°~0′, and the latitude there 41°~0′ (2205).

There comes out of the river of Balkh a river, its beginning is at longitude~91°~30′ and latitude~39°~40′ (2206) [1565]; it passes and cuts through a long mountain, and passes between the lower side of the city of Ushrūsana and the city of Khujanda, to a place whose longitude is 92°~30′ and latitude~34°~40′ (2207) [1566]; and passes at the corner of the city of al-Muḥammadiyya, and passes near \Ang{\langle}the city of\Ang{\rangle} Kirmān, and flows into the sea at longitude~87°~30′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~28°~40′\Ang{\rangle} (2208) [1567].

There falls into this river the river \textarabic{سليمس}, from a mountain; its beginning is at longitude~88°~0′ and latitude~36°~0′ (2209) [1568], and it flows \Ang{\langle}into the river\Ang{\rangle} \Ang{\langle}near\Ang{\rangle} al-Muḥammadiyya at longitude~89°~40′ and latitude~32°~0′ (2210) [1569].

A spring comes out of a mountain at longitude~99°~40′ and latitude~39°~0′ (2211) [1570], and passes near the city of the merchants, and passes to a place whose longitude is 100°~0′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2212) [1571], and passes on and flows into the river of Balkh at longitude~96°~40′ and latitude~44°~30′ (2213) [1572].

And there falls into this spring a river from a spring, its beginning is at longitude~101°~40′ and latitude~39°~20′ (2214) [1573]; it passes touching the city of the merchants and flows into the river of the spring at longitude~100°~0′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2215) [1574]\Pg{p. 147}.

And there also falls into this first spring a river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~84/104°~30′ and latitude~47°~5′ (2216) [1575], and its mouth is in the river of the spring at longitude~97°~20′ and latitude~44°~5′ (2217) [1576].

A long river\footnoteT{\textarabic{نهر طويل}, «a long river»; the following paragraphs call it «the great river».}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~129°~30′ and latitude~46°~0′ (2218) [1577]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 108°~0′ and latitude~46°~5′ (2219) [1578]; it passes to a place whose longitude is 107°~5′ and latitude~50°~30′ (2220) [1579]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 100°~30′ and latitude~51°~0′ (2221) [1580]; and passes near the city of the Khazars and near the sea, between the sea and the city of Khwārazm, and flows into the marsh of the river of Balkh at longitude~90°~5′ and latitude~41°~30′ (2222) [1581].

And there falls into this river a river from the mountain \textarabic{ارسانا}, its beginning is at longitude~99°~0′ and latitude~57°~30′ (2223) [1582], and its mouth is in the great river at longitude~92°~5′ and latitude~45°~5′ (2224) [1583].

And there falls into the great river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{ارسانا}, its beginning is at longitude~100°~0′ and latitude~19/59°~5′ (2225) [1584], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~94°~5′ and latitude~46°~5′ (2226) [1585].

And there falls into the great river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{اسقاسا}, its beginning is at longitude~104°~40′ and latitude~56°~5′ (2227) [1586], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~100°~30′ and latitude~51°~5′ (2228) [1587].

And there falls into the great river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{اسڡاسا}, its beginning is at longitude~106°~30′ and latitude~54°~5′ (2229) [1588], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~\Ang{\langle}104°\Ang{\rangle}~30′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}51°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (2230) [1589].

\Ang{\langle}And there falls\Ang{\rangle} into the great river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{اسڡاسا}, its beginning is \Ang{\langle}at longitude\Ang{\rangle}~108°~5′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}52°\Ang{\rangle}~30′ (2231) [1590], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~107°~20′ and latitude~50°~30′ (2232) [1591]\Pg{p. 148}.

And there also fall into the great river three rivers from a mountain: the source of the first river is \Ang{\langle}at longitude\Ang{\rangle}~108°~0′ and latitude~48°~30′ (2233), and its mouth is in the river at longitude~103°~10′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2234); the second, its beginning is at longitude~103°~30′ and latitude~47°~30′ (2235), and its mouth is in the river at longitude~104°~30′ and latitude~50°~40′ (2236); and the third, its beginning is at longitude~104°~45′ and latitude~48°~5′ (2237), and its mouth is in the river at longitude~106°~5′ and latitude~50°~20′ (2238).

And there also fall into the great river three rivers from the mountain \textarabic{طغورا}: the first of them, its source is at longitude~112°~0′ and latitude~58°~10′ (2239) [1592], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~109°~20′ and latitude~50°~5′ (2240) [1593]; the source of the second is at longitude~114°~5′ and latitude~52°~5′ (2241) [1594], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~112°~10′ and latitude~49°~30′ (2242) [1595]; and the source of the third is at longitude~115°~30′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2243) [1596], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~114°~5′ and latitude~48°~30′ (2244) [1597].

And there falls into this great river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~109°~40′ and latitude~44°~30′ (2245) [1598]; its mouth is in the river at longitude~118°~5′ and latitude~46°~5′ (2246) [1599].

And there falls into the great river also a spring, its beginning is at longitude~124°~30′ and latitude~49°~5′ (2247) [1600], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~123°~0′ and latitude~46°~20′ (2248) [1601].

The river \textarabic{بوخردس}, its beginning is a spring at longitude~145°~30′ and latitude~47°~0′ (2249) [1602]\Pg{p. 149}; it passes to a place whose longitude is 152°~30′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2250) [1603]; and passes and enters between the dam and the mountain of Gog at longitude~168°~30′ and latitude~50°~40′ (2251) [1604], and turns aside to the city of Magog, touching it, to a place whose longitude is 172°~0′ and latitude~62°~0′ (2252); and turns back to a place whose longitude is 172°~0′ and latitude~59°~0′ (2253) [1605]; \Ang{\langle}then\Ang{\rangle} it returns to a place whose longitude is 175°~0′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2254) [1606], and comes to a place whose longitude is 175°~0′ and latitude~56°~0′ (2255) [1607], and comes to longitude~180°~0′ and latitude~49°~30′ (2256) [1608].

There falls into this river a spring, its beginning is at longitude~147°~30′ and latitude~52°~30′ (2257) [1609], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~152°~40′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2258) [1610].

And there falls into this first river a river from the mountain \textarabic{اسمريا}, its beginning is at longitude~158°~0′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2259) [1611], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~158°~30′ and latitude~51°~0′ (2260) [1612].

A spring from the city of \textarabic{طامى}, its beginning is at longitude~25°~10′ and latitude~59°~20′ (2295) [2137], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~27°~20′ and latitude~58°~45′ (2296) [2138].

The river \textarabic{عاٮوس}, its beginning is at longitude~41°~30′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}52°\Ang{\rangle}~0′ (2313) [2149]; it passes touching the mountain \textarabic{مٮلٮاو}, and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~32°~0′ and latitude~59°~0′ (2314) [2150].

A spring from this mountain also, its beginning is at longitude~42°~40′ and latitude~55°~45′ (2321) [2157], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~41°~5′ and latitude~58°~0′ (2322) [2158]\Pg{p. 153}.

The river \textarabic{اسطولس} from the mountain \textarabic{سرطاميڡا}, its beginning is at longitude~43°~5′ and latitude~52°~5′ (2323) [2159]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~47°~0′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2324) [2160].

A spring that comes to the marsh \textarabic{راستاس}, its beginning is at longitude~59°~40′ and latitude~56°~20′ (2331) [2165], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~51°~0′ and latitude~54°~10′ (2332) [2166].

And the size of the marsh is half a degree, and its centre is at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~58°~50′ (2333) [1034].

And there also falls into this marsh a spring, its beginning is at longitude~52°~20′ and latitude~55°~5′ (2334) [2167], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~54°~10′ (2335) [2168].

A river that flows into the sea comes out of this marsh; \Ang{\langle}its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~51°~30′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2336) [2169], and its mouth is \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the sea at longitude~51°~0′ and latitude~58°~40′ (2337) [2170].

Out of the river of this spring there comes a river, its beginning is at longitude~51°~10′ and latitude~57°~30′ (2338) [2171], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~58°~0′ and latitude~59°~0′ (2339) [2172].

\Pg{p. 154}A spring \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the island of \textarabic{ارماٮوس} of the women, its beginning is at longitude~52°~20′ and latitude~60°~40′ (2340) [2173], and its mouth is \Ang{\langle}in\Ang{\rangle} the sea at longitude~57°~30′ and latitude~64°~0′ (2341) [2174]. Out of the river of this spring there come two rivers: the first of them, its source is at longitude~52°~40′ and latitude~61°~30′ (2342) [2175], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~58°~20′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}58°\Ang{\rangle}~0′ (2343) [2176]; and the source of the second river is at longitude~54°~30′ and latitude~62°~30′ (2344) [2177], and its mouth is in \Ang{\langle}the sea\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~54°~10′ and latitude~64°~10′ (2345) [2178].

A spring in the island of \textarabic{امراٮوس} of the men, its beginning is at longitude~54°~40′ and latitude~65°~40′ (2346) [2179]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~50°~0′ and latitude~68°~0′ (2347) [2180]. Out of the river of this spring there come two rivers whose beginning is one place, at longitude~52°~30′ and latitude~65°~0′ (2348) [2181]; the mouth of one of them is in the sea at longitude~50°~30′ and latitude~65°~0′ (2349) [2182], and the mouth of the other in the sea at longitude~58°~40′ and latitude~66°~30′ (2350) [2183].

Two rivers from a mountain: the source of one of them is at longitude~54°~0′ and latitude~56°~30′ (2351) [2184], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~54°~0′ and latitude~59°~10′ (2352) [2185]; and the source of the second river is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}54°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}10′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~56°~30′ (2353) [2186], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~57°~40′ and latitude~59°~25′ (2354) [2187].

\Pg{p. 155}And four rivers come to the marsh from a mountain. \Ang{\langle}And the first river\Ang{\rangle}: its source is \Ang{\langle}at longitude\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}58°\Ang{\rangle}~10′ and latitude~58°~10′ (2355) [2188], and its mouth is in the marsh at \Ang{\langle}longitude~58°~45′ and latitude~54°~20′\Ang{\rangle} (2356) [2189]. \Ang{\langle}And the\Ang{\rangle} fourth \Ang{\langle}river\Ang{\rangle}: its source is from longitude~58°~10′ and latitude~54°~30′ (2357) [2190]; its mouth is in the sea at longitude~\Ang{\langle}58°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~55°~20′ (2358) [2191]. And the two other rivers lie between them, at equal distances in longitude and latitude, so that there are four equal, straight rivers.

A spring that comes to this marsh \Ang{\langle}also; its beginning is\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~56°~40′ and latitude~56°~50′ (2359) [2192], and its mouth is in the marsh at \Ang{\langle}longitude~62°~0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~56°~30′ (2360) [2193].

The river \textarabic{طابس}, its beginning is from a spring at longitude~68°~30′ and latitude~60°~20′ (2361) [2194]; it passes to the corner of the city of \textarabic{سورس} and turns back and flows into the marsh at longitude~66°~\Ang{\langle}30′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~57°~30′ (2362) [2195]. A river comes out of this river, its beginning is at longitude~68°~0′ and latitude~58°~0′ (2363) [2196], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~67°~30′ and latitude~57°~20′ (2364) [2197].

The river \textarabic{مرونس} comes out of the mountain \textarabic{اڡقا}, its beginning is at longitude~72°~40′ and latitude~56°~30′ (2365) [2198], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~\Ang{\langle}69°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}40′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~55°~50′ (2366) [2199].

The river \textarabic{بوفاس} from the mountain \textarabic{افقا} \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is at longitude~72°~30′ and latitude~56°~0′ (2367) [2200], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~69°~30′ and latitude~55°~20′ (2368) [2201].

The river \textarabic{اسطس} from the mountain \textarabic{اڡيڡا} \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is at longitude~72°~30′ and latitude~55°~30′ (2369) [2202]\Pg{p. 156}, and its mouth is in the marsh at \Ang{\langle}longitude~69°~30′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~54°~45′ (2370) [2203].

The river \textarabic{اطفطيس} from the mountain \textarabic{اڡيفا} \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is at longitude~78°~0′ and latitude~54°~0′ (2371) [2204], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~69°~30′ and latitude~54°~20′ (2372) [2205].

The river \textarabic{اٮحارس} from the mountain \textarabic{اڡٮڡا} \Ang{\langle}also\Ang{\rangle}, its beginning is at longitude~74°~0′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2373) [2206], and its mouth is in the marsh at longitude~69°~0′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}53°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}55′\Ang{\rangle} (2374) [2207].

Two rivers come out of this marsh. The beginning of one of them is at longitude~62°~0′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}56°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}30′\Ang{\rangle} (2375) [2208]; it passes \Ang{\langle}and cuts through\Ang{\rangle} the mountain \textarabic{دقيا}, and passes on and flows into \Ang{\langle}the sea\Ang{\rangle} at \Ang{\langle}longitude\Ang{\rangle}~59°~20′ and latitude~60°~20′ (2376) [2209]. And the second river, its beginning is at longitude~68°~0′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~57°~40′\Ang{\rangle} (2377) [2210]; it passes and cuts through the mountain \textarabic{دقيا}, and passes on and flows into the sea at longitude~60°~30′ and latitude~60°~30′ (2378) [2211].

Two rivers come out of the mountain \textarabic{فوقا}, \Ang{\langle}pass by\Ang{\rangle} the city of \textarabic{طرمى} \Ang{\langle}touching it\Ang{\rangle}, and both flow into the marsh; and the city is on the marsh.

\Ang{\langle}This\Ang{\rangle} marsh: its beginning is from longitude~58°~\Ang{\langle}45′\Ang{\rangle} (2379) [1035] \Ang{\langle}to longitude\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}59°\Ang{\rangle}~40′ (2380) [1036]\Pg{p. 157}. It begins from the place of the channel of the four rivers, in the measure of the breadth of the four \Ang{\langle}rivers . . . . . .\Ang{\rangle} the two sides, until it comes to that one of its sides which is next to the sea, at longitude~68°~30′ \Ang{\langle}and latitude~52°~30′\Ang{\rangle} (2381); \Ang{\langle}then\Ang{\rangle} the marsh \Ang{\langle}turns\Ang{\rangle} and passes by the city of \textarabic{طرمى}, and passes \Ang{\langle}by the five rivers\Ang{\rangle} whose mouth is at longitude~69°~0′ (2382) and whose beginning is from the mountain \textarabic{افقا}, until it reaches the corner of the city of \Ang{\langle}\textarabic{طاس}\Ang{\rangle}; then it passes to a place whose longitude is 68°~40′ and latitude~57°~40′ (2383), and passes to a place whose longitude is 62°~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~55°~30′ (2384) [1037]; then it passes to a place below the channel of the four rivers. Then two rivers come out of it between the two seas, and their mouths are in the Sea of the North. And this is the picture of that.

\Ang{\langle}The river \textarabic{را}: its beginning is from a mountain at longitude~72°~20′ and latitude~60°~30′\Ang{\rangle} (2385) [2212]; \Ang{\langle}it passes to a place\Ang{\rangle} whose longitude is 81°~30′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}58°~30′\Ang{\rangle} (2386) [2213]; and passes to a place whose longitude is 75°~40′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}57°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}20′\Ang{\rangle} (2387) [2214]; \Ang{\langle}and passes and strikes\Ang{\rangle} one of the two towers \Ang{\langle}that\Ang{\rangle} Dhū l-Qarnayn \Ang{\langle}built\Ang{\rangle}, and passes on and flows into the sea at longitude~\Ang{\langle}81°\Ang{\rangle}~10′ and latitude~49°~20′ (2388) [2215].

\Ang{\langle}And there falls into\Ang{\rangle} this river a river from a mountain, its beginning is at longitude~92°~30′ and latitude~61°~0′ (2389) [2216], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~81°~30′ and latitude~58°~30′ (2390) [2217].

\Pg{p. 158}Two rivers from the mountain \textarabic{ڡرمانيا}: the beginning of one of them is at longitude~\Ang{\langle}78°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} and latitude~51°~0′ (2391) [2218], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~79°~30′ and latitude~48°~30′ (2392) [2219]; and the source of the second is at longitude~78°~40′ and latitude~51°~30′ (2393) [2220], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~80°~40′ and latitude~\Ang{\langle}49°\Ang{\rangle}~\Ang{\langle}0′\Ang{\rangle} (2394) [2221].

A river from the mountain \textarabic{روميقا}, its beginning is at longitude~88°~0′ and latitude~57°~0′ (2395) [2222], and its mouth is in \Ang{\langle}the sea\Ang{\rangle} at longitude~84°~0′ and latitude~50°~20′ (2396) [2223].

A river from the mountain \textarabic{زوميڡا}, its beginning is at longitude~89°~0′ and latitude~56°~45′ (2397) [2224], and its mouth is in the sea at longitude~85°~30′ and latitude~51°~0′ (2398) [2225].

There falls into it a river from the mountain \textarabic{روميڡا}, its beginning is at longitude~91°~0′ and latitude~56°~5′ (2399) [2226], and its mouth is in the river at longitude~88°~0′ and latitude~54°~0′ (2400) [2227].

And there falls into the first river also a river from the mountain \textarabic{ارساٮا}, its beginning is at longitude~97°~0′ and latitude~56°~0′ (2401) [2228], and its mouth is in the first river at longitude~87°~20′ and latitude~58°~0′ (2402) [2229].
